"priority": "medium", "response_data": "{\"chunks\": [{\"byte_end\": 11567, \"byte_start\": 0, \"chunk_index\": 0, \"content\": \"\\n# \\ud83d\\udd2c Research: Reminder System Architecture Analysis \\u2014 scribe_tool_output_refinement\\n**Author:** ResearchAgent\\n**Version:** v1.0\\n**Status:** Complete\\n**Last Updated:** 2026-01-03 11:44:30 UTC\\n\\n> This document provides comprehensive analysis of the current reminder system architecture to inform DB-integrated, session-aware redesign. It examines current file-based cooldown mechanisms, session tracking capabilities, database infrastructure readiness, and tool integration patterns to identify gaps and design opportunities for session-isolated reminder deduplication.\\n\\n---\\n## Executive Summary\\n<!-- ID: executive_summary -->\\n**Primary Objective:** Conduct comprehensive analysis of the current reminder system to inform DB-integrated, session-aware redesign that enables proper session isolation and failure-triggered reminder prioritization.\\n\\n**Key Takeaways:**\\n- **Critical Gap Identified:** Current file-based cooldown cache (`data/reminder_cooldowns.json`) lacks session isolation - reminders persist across all sessions for the same project+agent+tool combination\\n- **Infrastructure Ready:** Robust session tracking already exists in DB (`agent_sessions`, `scribe_sessions` tables) with session_id support in `execution_context.py`\\n- **Novel Enhancement:** No prior session-aware caching work exists across Scribe projects - this will be pioneering implementation\\n- **Missing Table:** `reminder_history` table needs to be added to SQLite schema for DB-integrated cooldown tracking\\n- **Tool Integration Gap:** No distinction between success/failure context when generating reminders - all reminders treated equally\\n- **Singleton Pattern:** Global `_reminder_engine` instance shared across all sessions prevents per-session state management\\n\\n\\n---\\n## Research Scope\\n<!-- ID: research_scope -->\\n**Research Lead:** ResearchAgent\\n\\n**Investigation Window:** 2026-01-03 11:40 \\u2014 2026-01-03 11:44 UTC\\n\\n**Focus Areas:**\\n- \\u2705 Current reminder architecture (`utils/reminder_engine.py`)\\n- \\u2705 Cooldown cache persistence mechanism (file-based JSON)\\n- \\u2705 Session tracking infrastructure (`shared/execution_context.py`)\\n- \\u2705 Database schema analysis (`storage/sqlite.py`)\\n- \\u2705 Tool integration patterns (append_entry, read_file)\\n- \\u2705 Cross-project learning (session-aware caching patterns)\\n\\n**Dependencies & Constraints:**\\n- **Existing Infrastructure:** Must integrate with existing session tracking (`agent_sessions`, `scribe_sessions` tables)\\n- **Backwards Compatibility:** File-based cooldown cache currently contains 47KB of data from test runs and production usage\\n- **Singleton Architecture:** Current global singleton pattern conflicts with per-session state requirements\\n- **10-Minute Context Window:** Design must support detection and reset of 10-minute context boundaries\\n\\n\\n---\\n## Findings\\n<!-- ID: findings -->\\n\\n### Finding 1: File-Based Cooldown Mechanism Lacks Session Isolation\\n- **Summary:** Current implementation uses composite hash keys (`project_root agent_id tool_name reminder_key`) that persist across all sessions, preventing proper session-aware cooldown isolation\\n- **Evidence:**\\n  - `utils/reminder_engine.py:282-287` - `_get_reminder_hash()` constructs keys without session_id component\\n  - `utils/reminder_engine.py:236-255` - `_save_cooldown_cache()` persists to `data/reminder_cooldowns.json` with atomic write pattern\\n  - `data/reminder_cooldowns.json` - Real-world file contains 47KB of entries with format: `{project_root} {agent_id} {tool_name} {reminder_key}: {timestamp}`\\n- **Confidence:** 0.97 (High)\\n\\n### Finding 2: Robust Session Tracking Infrastructure Already Exists\\n- **Summary:** Database contains comprehensive session tracking tables (`agent_sessions`, `scribe_sessions`, `session_projects`) with TTL, identity_key, and transport_session_id mapping capabilities\\n- **Evidence:**\\n  - `storage/sqlite.py:681-692` - `agent_sessions` table schema with identity_key, TTL, expires_at fields\\n  - `storage/sqlite.py:740-748` - `scribe_sessions` table with transport_session_id \\u2192 stable session_id mapping\\n  - `shared/execution_context.py:62-112` - `get_or_create_session_id()` implements 3-tier lookup: memory cache \\u2192 DB \\u2192 create new\\n- **Confidence:** 0.96 (High)\\n\\n### Finding 3: Missing reminder_history Table in Database\\n- **Summary:** No `reminder_history` table exists in current SQLite schema - all reminder cooldown tracking is file-based only\\n- **Evidence:**\\n  - Searched `storage/sqlite.py` for `CREATE TABLE` statements - found `agent_sessions`, `scribe_sessions`, `session_projects`, `agent_projects`, but no reminder-related tables\\n  - Grep for `reminder` in CREATE TABLE context returned no matches\\n- **Confidence:** 1.0 (Absolute)\\n\\n### Finding 4: Singleton Pattern Prevents Per-Session State\\n- **Summary:** Global `_reminder_engine` instance in `reminders.py` is shared across all sessions, preventing session-specific cooldown state management\\n- **Evidence:**\\n  - `reminders.py:20-28` - `_get_engine()` returns global singleton instance\\n  - `reminder_engine.py:66-87` - `ReminderEngine.__init__()` initializes single `ReminderHistory` object for all sessions\\n- **Confidence:** 0.93 (High)\\n\\n### Finding 5: No Success/Failure Context in Reminder Generation\\n- **Summary:** Tools call `get_reminders()` after main execution completes, but no context is passed about operation outcome (success vs failure) to enable failure-triggered priority reminders\\n- **Evidence:**\\n  - `tools/append_entry.py:1210-1212` - `get_reminders()` called after entry writing completes\\n  - `tools/read_file.py:502-517` - Reminders generated regardless of read operation outcome\\n  - No status/success parameter passed to `get_reminders()` function\\n- **Confidence:** 0.91 (High)\\n\\n### Finding 6: Session Age Tracking Exists But Is Unused\\n- **Summary:** Session age is calculated from `state.session_started_at` but not integrated into cooldown hash or reset logic\\n- **Evidence:**\\n  - `reminders.py:179-186` - `session_age_minutes` extracted from state in `_build_legacy_context()`\\n  - `reminder_engine.py:54` - `ReminderContext` includes `session_age_minutes` field\\n  - No usage of `session_age_minutes` in cooldown reset or hash generation logic\\n- **Confidence:** 0.88 (High)\\n\\n### Finding 7: Cross-Project Search Revealed No Prior Work\\n- **Summary:** No existing implementations of session-aware cooldown caching exist across all Scribe projects - this redesign will be pioneering work\\n- **Evidence:**\\n  - `query_entries(search_scope='all_projects', message='session cooldown cache isolation')` - No results\\n  - `query_entries(search_scope='all_projects', message='reminder deduplication session-aware')` - No results\\n- **Confidence:** 0.88 (Medium-High - absence of evidence)\\n\\n\\n---\\n## Technical Analysis\\n<!-- ID: technical_analysis -->\\n\\n### Code Patterns Identified\\n\\n**Current Architecture:**\\n```\\n\\u250c\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2510\\n\\u2502 Tool Execution (append_entry, read_file, etc.)             \\u2502\\n\\u2502   \\u2514\\u2500> get_reminders(project, tool_name, state)             \\u2502\\n\\u2502        \\u2514\\u2500> _build_legacy_context()                         \\u2502\\n\\u2502             \\u2514\\u2500> ReminderEngine.generate_reminders()        \\u2502\\n\\u2502                  \\u2514\\u2500> _get_reminder_hash()                  \\u2502\\n\\u2502                       \\u2514\\u2500> f\\\"{project_root} {agent_id}      \\u2502\\n\\u2502                             {tool_name} {reminder_key}\\\"    \\u2502\\n\\u2502                            (NO SESSION_ID)                 \\u2502\\n\\u2502   Cooldown Cache: data/reminder_cooldowns.json            \\u2502\\n\\u2502   Session Tracking: execution_context.py (UNUSED)         \\u2502\\n\\u2514\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2518\\n```\\n\\n**Hash Generation Pattern (Current):**\\n- Format: `{project_root} {agent_id} {tool_name} {reminder_key}`\\n- Persisted to: `data/reminder_cooldowns.json`\\n- Lifetime: 24 hours (configurable via `cooldown_cache_retention_minutes`)\\n- Atomic writes: Uses `.tmp` file then `replace()` for safety\\n\\n**Session Tracking Pattern (Existing but Unused):**\\n- `ExecutionContext.session_id` - Stable UUID persisted across restarts\\n- `ExecutionContext.transport_session_id` - Unstable ID from MCP transport\\n- `RouterContextManager.get_or_create_session_id()` - 3-tier lookup with DB persistence\\n- Tables: `agent_sessions`, `scribe_sessions`, `session_projects`\\n\\n### System Interactions\\n\\n**Current Dependencies:**\\n1. **Reminder Engine \\u2192 File System**\\n   - Reads/writes `data/reminder_cooldowns.json`\\n   - Atomic write pattern with `.tmp` file\\n   - 24-hour cleanup via `_cleanup_cooldown_cache()`\\n\\n2. **Execution Context \\u2192 Database**\\n   - `upsert_session()` persists session to `scribe_sessions` table\\n   - `get_session_by_transport()` retrieves stable session_id from transport_session_id\\n   - Session heartbeat updates `last_active_at` timestamp\\n\\n3. **Tool Layer \\u2192 Reminder Engine**\\n   - Post-execution call to `get_reminders()`\\n   - No feedback about operation success/failure\\n   - State object passed contains `session_started_at` but no session_id\\n\\n**Missing Integration:**\\n- Reminder engine does NOT query database for session info\\n- Session_id NOT included in cooldown hash keys\\n- No link between `reminder_history` and `agent_sessions` tables\\n\\n### Risk Assessment\\n\\n**Technical Risks:**\\n- \\u2705 **File-Based Race Conditions:** Atomic write pattern (`.tmp` + `replace()`) mitigates concurrent write risks\\n- \\u26a0\\ufe0f **Singleton Shared State:** Global `_reminder_engine` means concurrent sessions share cooldown state (HIGH RISK)\\n- \\u26a0\\ufe0f **Test Data Pollution:** `data/reminder_cooldowns.json` contains test project UUIDs mixed with production data (MEDIUM RISK)\\n- \\u2705 **Database Session Infrastructure:** Existing tables and methods are production-ready (LOW RISK)\\n- \\u26a0\\ufe0f **Migration Complexity:** Moving from file-based to DB-based cooldowns requires careful data migration (MEDIUM RISK)\\n\\n\\n---\\n## Recommendations\\n<!-- ID: recommendations -->\\n\\n### Immediate Next Steps\\n\\n**Architecture Phase:**\\n1. **Design `reminder_history` Table Schema**\\n   - Columns: `id`, `session_id`, `project_root`, `agent_id`, `tool_name`, `reminder_key`, `shown_at`, `context` (success/failure)\\n   - Indexes: `(session_id, reminder_key)`, `(shown_at)` for TTL cleanup\\n   - Foreign key: `session_id` \\u2192 `scribe_sessions.session_id` (ON DELETE CASCADE)\\n\\n2. **Define Session Reset Strategy**\\n   - **Option A: Time-Based (10-minute idle)** - Use `agent_sessions.last_active_at` to detect context boundaries\\n   - **Option B: Context-Based** - Explicit session end events trigger cooldown reset\\n   - **Recommendation:** Hybrid approach - reset on 10-minute idle OR explicit session end\\n\\n3. **Specify Failure-Triggered Reminder Priority**\\n   - Add `operation_status` parameter to `get_reminders()` function\\n   - Prioritize failure-context reminders (e.g., \\\"no logs\\\" after failed `append_entry`)\\n   - Lower priority for success-context reminders\\n\\n**Implementation Phase:**\\n4. **Refactor Hash Generation**\\n   - Include `session_id` in cooldown hash: `{session_id} {project_root} {agent_id} {tool_name} {reminder_key}`\\n   - Update `_get_reminder_hash()` to accept `session_id` parameter\\n\\n5. **Create DB Storage Methods**\\n   - `upsert_reminder_shown(session_id, reminder_key, context)` - Record reminder display\\n   - `check_reminder_cooldown(session_id, reminder_key, cooldown_minutes)` - Query recent displays\\n\", read_file   format_requested=readable; response_data={\"chunks\": [{\"byte_end\": 11567, \"byte_start\": 0, \"chunk_index\": 0, \"content\": \"\\n# \\ud83d\\udd2c Research: Reminder System Architecture Analysis \\u2014 scribe_tool_output_refinement\\n**Author:** ResearchAgent\\n**Version:** v1.0\\n**Status:** Complete\\n**Last Updated:** 2026-01-03 11:44:30 UTC\\n\\n> This document provides comprehensive analysis of the current reminder system architecture to inform DB-integrated, session-aware redesign. It examines current file-based cooldown mechanisms, session tracking capabilities, database infrastructure readiness, and tool integration patterns to identify gaps and design opportunities for session-isolated reminder deduplication.\\n\\n---\\n## Executive Summary\\n<!-- ID: executive_summary -->\\n**Primary Objective:** Conduct comprehensive analysis of the current reminder system to inform DB-integrated, session-aware redesign that enables proper session isolation and failure-triggered reminder prioritization.\\n\\n**Key Takeaways:**\\n- **Critical Gap Identified:** Current file-based cooldown cache (`data/reminder_cooldowns.json`) lacks session isolation - reminders persist across all sessions for the same project+agent+tool combination\\n- **Infrastructure Ready:** Robust session tracking already exists in DB (`agent_sessions`, `scribe_sessions` tables) with session_id support in `execution_context.py`\\n- **Novel Enhancement:** No prior session-aware caching work exists across Scribe projects - this will be pioneering implementation\\n- **Missing Table:** `reminder_history` table needs to be added to SQLite schema for DB-integrated cooldown tracking\\n- **Tool Integration Gap:** No distinction between success/failure context when generating reminders - all reminders treated equally\\n- **Singleton Pattern:** Global `_reminder_engine` instance shared across all sessions prevents per-session state management\\n\\n\\n---\\n## Research Scope\\n<!-- ID: research_scope -->\\n**Research Lead:** ResearchAgent\\n\\n**Investigation Window:** 2026-01-03 11:40 \\u2014 2026-01-03 11:44 UTC\\n\\n**Focus Areas:**\\n- \\u2705 Current reminder architecture (`utils/reminder_engine.py`)\\n- \\u2705 Cooldown cache persistence mechanism (file-based JSON)\\n- \\u2705 Session tracking infrastructure (`shared/execution_context.py`)\\n- \\u2705 Database schema analysis (`storage/sqlite.py`)\\n- \\u2705 Tool integration patterns (append_entry, read_file)\\n- \\u2705 Cross-project learning (session-aware caching patterns)\\n\\n**Dependencies & Constraints:**\\n- **Existing Infrastructure:** Must integrate with existing session tracking (`agent_sessions`, `scribe_sessions` tables)\\n- **Backwards Compatibility:** File-based cooldown cache currently contains 47KB of data from test runs and production usage\\n- **Singleton Architecture:** Current global singleton pattern conflicts with per-session state requirements\\n- **10-Minute Context Window:** Design must support detection and reset of 10-minute context boundaries\\n\\n\\n---\\n## Findings\\n<!-- ID: findings -->\\n\\n### Finding 1: File-Based Cooldown Mechanism Lacks Session Isolation\\n- **Summary:** Current implementation uses composite hash keys (`project_root agent_id tool_name reminder_key`) that persist across all sessions, preventing proper session-aware cooldown isolation\\n- **Evidence:**\\n  - `utils/reminder_engine.py:282-287` - `_get_reminder_hash()` constructs keys without session_id component\\n  - `utils/reminder_engine.py:236-255` - `_save_cooldown_cache()` persists to `data/reminder_cooldowns.json` with atomic write pattern\\n  - `data/reminder_cooldowns.json` - Real-world file contains 47KB of entries with format: `{project_root} {agent_id} {tool_name} {reminder_key}: {timestamp}`\\n- **Confidence:** 0.97 (High)\\n\\n### Finding 2: Robust Session Tracking Infrastructure Already Exists\\n- **Summary:** Database contains comprehensive session tracking tables (`agent_sessions`, `scribe_sessions`, `session_projects`) with TTL, identity_key, and transport_session_id mapping capabilities\\n- **Evidence:**\\n  - `storage/sqlite.py:681-692` - `agent_sessions` table schema with identity_key, TTL, expires_at fields\\n  - `storage/sqlite.py:740-748` - `scribe_sessions` table with transport_session_id \\u2192 stable session_id mapping\\n  - `shared/execution_context.py:62-112` - `get_or_create_session_id()` implements 3-tier lookup: memory cache \\u2192 DB \\u2192 create new\\n- **Confidence:** 0.96 (High)\\n\\n### Finding 3: Missing reminder_history Table in Database\\n- **Summary:** No `reminder_history` table exists in current SQLite schema - all reminder cooldown tracking is file-based only\\n- **Evidence:**\\n  - Searched `storage/sqlite.py` for `CREATE TABLE` statements - found `agent_sessions`, `scribe_sessions`, `session_projects`, `agent_projects`, but no reminder-related tables\\n  - Grep for `reminder` in CREATE TABLE context returned no matches\\n- **Confidence:** 1.0 (Absolute)\\n\\n### Finding 4: Singleton Pattern Prevents Per-Session State\\n- **Summary:** Global `_reminder_engine` instance in `reminders.py` is shared across all sessions, preventing session-specific cooldown state management\\n- **Evidence:**\\n  - `reminders.py:20-28` - `_get_engine()` returns global singleton instance\\n  - `reminder_engine.py:66-87` - `ReminderEngine.__init__()` initializes single `ReminderHistory` object for all sessions\\n- **Confidence:** 0.93 (High)\\n\\n### Finding 5: No Success/Failure Context in Reminder Generation\\n- **Summary:** Tools call `get_reminders()` after main execution completes, but no context is passed about operation outcome (success vs failure) to enable failure-triggered priority reminders\\n- **Evidence:**\\n  - `tools/append_entry.py:1210-1212` - `get_reminders()` called after entry writing completes\\n  - `tools/read_file.py:502-517` - Reminders generated regardless of read operation outcome\\n  - No status/success parameter passed to `get_reminders()` function\\n- **Confidence:** 0.91 (High)\\n\\n### Finding 6: Session Age Tracking Exists But Is Unused\\n- **Summary:** Session age is calculated from `state.session_started_at` but not integrated into cooldown hash or reset logic\\n- **Evidence:**\\n  - `reminders.py:179-186` - `session_age_minutes` extracted from state in `_build_legacy_context()`\\n  - `reminder_engine.py:54` - `ReminderContext` includes `session_age_minutes` field\\n  - No usage of `session_age_minutes` in cooldown reset or hash generation logic\\n- **Confidence:** 0.88 (High)\\n\\n### Finding 7: Cross-Project Search Revealed No Prior Work\\n- **Summary:** No existing implementations of session-aware cooldown caching exist across all Scribe projects - this redesign will be pioneering work\\n- **Evidence:**\\n  - `query_entries(search_scope='all_projects', message='session cooldown cache isolation')` - No results\\n  - `query_entries(search_scope='all_projects', message='reminder deduplication session-aware')` - No results\\n- **Confidence:** 0.88 (Medium-High - absence of evidence)\\n\\n\\n---\\n## Technical Analysis\\n<!-- ID: technical_analysis -->\\n\\n### Code Patterns Identified\\n\\n**Current Architecture:**\\n```\\n\\u250c\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2510\\n\\u2502 Tool Execution (append_entry, read_file, etc.)             \\u2502\\n\\u2502   \\u2514\\u2500> get_reminders(project, tool_name, state)             \\u2502\\n\\u2502        \\u2514\\u2500> _build_legacy_context()                         \\u2502\\n\\u2502             \\u2514\\u2500> ReminderEngine.generate_reminders()        \\u2502\\n\\u2502                  \\u2514\\u2500> _get_reminder_hash()                  \\u2502\\n\\u2502                       \\u2514\\u2500> f\\\"{project_root} {agent_id}      \\u2502\\n\\u2502                             {tool_name} {reminder_key}\\\"    \\u2502\\n\\u2502                            (NO SESSION_ID)                 \\u2502\\n\\u2502   Cooldown Cache: data/reminder_cooldowns.json            \\u2502\\n\\u2502   Session Tracking: execution_context.py (UNUSED)         \\u2502\\n\\u2514\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2500\\u2518\\n```\\n\\n**Hash Generation Pattern (Current):**\\n- Format: `{project_root} {agent_id} {tool_name} {reminder_key}`\\n- Persisted to: `data/reminder_cooldowns.json`\\n- Lifetime: 24 hours (configurable via `cooldown_cache_retention_minutes`)\\n- Atomic writes: Uses `.tmp` file then `replace()` for safety\\n\\n**Session Tracking Pattern (Existing but Unused):**\\n- `ExecutionContext.session_id` - Stable UUID persisted across restarts\\n- `ExecutionContext.transport_session_id` - Unstable ID from MCP transport\\n- `RouterContextManager.get_or_create_session_id()` - 3-tier lookup with DB persistence\\n- Tables: `agent_sessions`, `scribe_sessions`, `session_projects`\\n\\n### System Interactions\\n\\n**Current Dependencies:**\\n1. **Reminder Engine \\u2192 File System**\\n   - Reads/writes `data/reminder_cooldowns.json`\\n   - Atomic write pattern with `.tmp` file\\n   - 24-hour cleanup via `_cleanup_cooldown_cache()`\\n\\n2. **Execution Context \\u2192 Database**\\n   - `upsert_session()` persists session to `scribe_sessions` table\\n   - `get_session_by_transport()` retrieves stable session_id from transport_session_id\\n   - Session heartbeat updates `last_active_at` timestamp\\n\\n3. **Tool Layer \\u2192 Reminder Engine**\\n   - Post-execution call to `get_reminders()`\\n   - No feedback about operation success/failure\\n   - State object passed contains `session_started_at` but no session_id\\n\\n**Missing Integration:**\\n- Reminder engine does NOT query database for session info\\n- Session_id NOT included in cooldown hash keys\\n- No link between `reminder_history` and `agent_sessions` tables\\n\\n### Risk Assessment\\n\\n**Technical Risks:**\\n- \\u2705 **File-Based Race Conditions:** Atomic write pattern (`.tmp` + `replace()`) mitigates concurrent write risks\\n- \\u26a0\\ufe0f **Singleton Shared State:** Global `_reminder_engine` means concurrent sessions share cooldown state (HIGH RISK)\\n- \\u26a0\\ufe0f **Test Data Pollution:** `data/reminder_cooldowns.json` contains test project UUIDs mixed with production data (MEDIUM RISK)\\n- \\u2705 **Database Session Infrastructure:** Existing tables and methods are production-ready (LOW RISK)\\n- \\u26a0\\ufe0f **Migration Complexity:** Moving from file-based to DB-based cooldowns requires careful data migration (MEDIUM RISK)\\n\\n\\n---\\n## Recommendations\\n<!-- ID: recommendations -->\\n\\n### Immediate Next Steps\\n\\n**Architecture Phase:**\\n1. **Design `reminder_history` Table Schema**\\n   - Columns: `id`, `session_id`, `project_root`, `agent_id`, `tool_name`, `reminder_key`, `shown_at`, `context` (success/failure)\\n   - Indexes: `(session_id, reminder_key)`, `(shown_at)` for TTL cleanup\\n   - Foreign key: `session_id` \\u2192 `scribe_sessions.session_id` (ON DELETE CASCADE)\\n\\n2. **Define Session Reset Strategy**\\n   - **Option A: Time-Based (10-minute idle)** - Use `agent_sessions.last_active_at` to detect context boundaries\\n   - **Option B: Context-Based** - Explicit session end events trigger cooldown reset\\n   - **Recommendation:** Hybrid approach - reset on 10-minute idle OR explicit session end\\n\\n3. **Specify Failure-Triggered Reminder Priority**\\n   - Add `operation_status` parameter to `get_reminders()` function\\n   - Prioritize failure-context reminders (e.g., \\\"no logs\\\" after failed `append_entry`)\\n   - Lower priority for success-context reminders\\n\\n**Implementation Phase:**\\n4. **Refactor Hash Generation**\\n   - Include `session_id` in cooldown hash: `{session_id} {project_root} {agent_id} {tool_name} {reminder_key}`\\n   - Update `_get_reminder_hash()` to accept `session_id` parameter\\n\\n5. **Create DB Storage Methods**\\n   - `upsert_reminder_shown(session_id, reminder_key, context)` - Record reminder display\\n   - `check_reminder_cooldown(session_id, reminder_key, cooldown_minutes)` - Query recent displays\\n\",
